Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\n\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Also added --max_steps, --kl_coef (beta).
